 18|def should_end_multi_loop(_react_loop_count, msg: AIMessage | None) -> bool:
 19|    if not isinstance(msg, AIMessage):
 20|        raise Exception(f"一次ainvoke调用后最新的图消息不是AIMessage，而是: {type(msg)}")
 21|
 22|    # 检查是否为 TASK_DONE（任务成功完成）
 23|    if is_task_done(msg):
 24|        logger.info(
 25|            "\n" + "=" * 41 + f" ✅ Team Leader 输出 {TASK_DONE_IDENTIFIER}，开发团队认为软件已可交付！" + "=" * 41 + "\n")
 26|        should_break_ = True
 27|        return should_break_
 28|
 29|    # 检查是否为 TASK_REJECTED（请求被拒绝，与软件开发需求无关）
 30|    if is_task_rejected(msg):
 31|        logger.warning(
 32|            "\n" + "=" * 41 + f" ⚠️  Team Leader 输出 {TASK_REJECTED_IDENTIFIER}，用户输入与软件开发需求无关or输入的软件开发需求被认为太过模糊，请求被拒绝！" + "=" * 41 + "\n")
 33|        should_break_ = True
 34|        return should_break_
 35|
 36|    # 检查是否为 EXCEPTION（请求被拒绝，与软件开发需求无关）
 37|    if is_task_rejected(msg):
 38|        logger.warning(
 39|            "\n" + "=" * 41 + f" ⚠️  Team Leader 输出 {EXCEPTION_IDENTIFIER}，出现异常情况！" + "=" * 41 + "\n")
 40|        should_break_ = True
 41|        return should_break_
 42|
 43|    if _react_loop_count > MAX_REACT_LOOP_COUNT:
 44|        logger.error(
 45|            "\n" + "=" * 41 + f" ❌ 开发团队已运行 {MAX_REACT_LOOP_COUNT} 轮 ReAct 循环，失败退出！" + "=" * 41 + "\n")
 46|
 47|        should_break_ = True
 48|        return should_break_
 49|
 50|    return False
 51|
 52|
 53|# 异步版本
 54|async def multi_react_loop(task: Task,
 55|                           dev_team: AgentTeam,
 56|                           recursion_limit: int = 10_000):
 57|    """
 58|    基于agentbay_based_dev_team的异步自动化软件开发
 59|    
 60|    Args:
 61|        task: 软件开发任务
 62|        dev_team: 开发团队实例（带MCP工具）
 63|        recursion_limit: 递归限制
 64|        enable_brown: 是否复用已有thread_id，启用棕地开发，
 65|
 66|    :raises: Exception - 任务执行过程中发生的异常
 67|    """
 68|    # 2 设定运行时配置
 69|    task_name = task.name
 70|    task_description = task.description_CN if task.description_CN else task.description
 71|    task_working_dir = dev_team.task_working_dir
 72|
 73|    thread_id = dev_team.thread_id
 74|    is_brown = dev_team.is_brown
 75|
 76|    config_across_react_loop: RunnableConfig = {
 77|        "tags": [f"{task_name}", f"{dev_team.team_type}"],
 78|        "recursion_limit": recursion_limit,
 79|        "configurable": {
 80|            "thread_id": thread_id,
 81|            "checkpoint_ns": dev_team.team_type  # ⭐ 可选：为 Leader/Planner 设置 namespace
 82|        }
 83|    }
 84|
 85|    input_text = None
 86|    react_loop_count = 1
 87|    latest_AI_message = None
 88|
 89|    while True:
 90|        # 第一次用户输入
 91|        if input_text is None:
 92|            if is_brown:
 93|                # 真正进行棕地开发
 94|                input_text = (f"棕地开发！继续完成未完成的"基础版"软件功能点\n"
 95|                              f"软件需求描述如下：\n"
 96|                              f"task_name:{task_name}\n"
 97|                              f"task_description:{task_description}\n"
 98|                              f"项目工作空间为：{task_working_dir}\n")
 99|            else:
100|                # 绿地开发
101|                input_text = (f"针对一个软件需求完成开发。\n"
102|                              f"软件需求描述如下：\n"
103|                              f"task_name:{task_name}\n"
104|                              f"task_description:{task_description}\n"
105|                              f"项目工作空间为：{task_working_dir}")
106|
107|
108|        else:
109|            # 或者上个ReAct循环结束后，等待用户本轮输入
110|            react_loop_count = react_loop_count + 1
111|            should_break = should_end_multi_loop(react_loop_count, latest_AI_message)
112|            if should_break:
113|                break
114|
115|            if dev_team.team_type == "simple":
116|                input_text = f"若当前软件已经完成"基础版"下所有软件功能点的开发，则仅回复'{TASK_DONE_IDENTIFIER}'，否则继续开发。"
117|            else:
118|                input_text = f"若当前软件已满足原始需求，则仅仅回复'{TASK_DONE_IDENTIFIER}'，否则继续开发。"
119|
120|        dev_input = {"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": input_text}]}
121|
122|        current_timestamp = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M")
123|        run_config = {
124|            **config_across_react_loop,
125|            "run_name": f'{task_name}_{react_loop_count} {current_timestamp}'
126|        }
127|
128|        logger.info("=" * 41)
129|        logger.info(f" 开启第 {react_loop_count} 轮 ReAct 循环: {task_name}_{react_loop_count} ")
130|        logger.info("=" * 41)
131|
132|        # 使用异步调用（一次ainvoke就是一个完整的ReAct loop）
133|        result = await dev_team.ainvoke(input=dev_input, config=run_config)
134|
135|        # 从返回结果中获取messages, 取最新AIMessage
136|        if result["messages"]:
137|            latest_msg = result['messages'][-1]
138|            if isinstance(latest_msg, AIMessage):
139|                latest_AI_message = latest_msg
140|            else:
141|                raise RuntimeError(f"一次ainvoke调用后最新的图消息不是AIMessage，而是: {type(latest_msg)}")
142|
143|
144|# 一次自动化开发
145|async def auto_dev(task: Task, team_type, enable_brown=False):
146|    """
147|    异步执行一次自动化软件开发任务（使用带MCP工具的DevTeam）
148|
149|    Args:
150|        task: 软件开发任务
151|        enable_brown: 是否复用旧thread_id
152|        :param team_type: 团队类型
153|    """
154|    # 记录开始时间
155|    start_time = time.time()
156|
157|    # 为每个任务配置独立的日志系统
158|    setup_logging(task_name=task.name, team_type=team_type, log_level=logging.INFO)
159|
160|    try:
161|        async with await AgentTeam.create_with_agentbay_mcp(
162|                task_name=task.name,
163|                team_type=team_type,
164|                enable_brown=enable_brown
165|        ) as dev_team:
166|
167|            # 开启多轮react loop
168|            await multi_react_loop(
169|                task,
170|                dev_team,
171|                recursion_limit=10_000,  # 为每个智能体设定的顶层节点步数
172|            )
173|
174|            # 计算耗时
175|            elapsed_time = time.time() - start_time
176|            return {
177|                'task_name': task.name,
178|                'success': True,
179|                'elapsed_time': elapsed_time
180|            }
181|    except Exception as e:
182|        if "沙盒会话已被主动提前断开" in str(e) or "沙盒会话即将达到最大运行时间限制" in str(e):
183|            # 捕获并打印异常
184|            logger.error(f"❌ 本次软件开发任务{task.name}运行时间过长，沙盒自动释放了。", exc_info=True)
185|            return {'task_name': task.name, 'success': False, 'elapsed_time': time.time() - start_time}
186|        else:
187|            raise e

## 17. src/utils/graph_running.py (283行)

```python
  1|import logging
  2|from typing import cast
  3|
  4|from langchain_core.runnables import RunnableConfig
  5|from langgraph.graph.state import CompiledStateGraph
  6|from langgraph.types import Interrupt, Command, StreamPart
  7|
  8|from src.dev_team.my_backend import LinuxSandboxBackend
  9|from src.dev_team.team import AgentTeam
 10|
 11|logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 12|
 13|
 14|def get_human_decisions(input_interrupt: Interrupt) -> list[dict]:
 15|    interrupt_value = input_interrupt.value
 16|    action_requests = interrupt_value["action_requests"]
 17|    review_configs = interrupt_value["review_configs"]
 18|
 19|    # Create a lookup map from tool name to review config
 20|    config_map = {cfg["action_name"]: cfg for cfg in review_configs}
 21|
 22|    # Display the pending actions to the user
 23|    # 并发工具调用下，可能会有多个工具调用被中断，这些中断需要人类顺序逐个处理
 24|    user_decisions = []
 25|    for action in action_requests:
 26|        review_config = config_map[action["name"]]
 27|        print(f"Tool: {action['name']}")
 28|        print(f"Arguments: {action['args']}")
 29|        print(f"Allowed decisions: {review_config['allowed_decisions']}")
 30|        decision_type = input("\nEnter your decision('a'-approve/'r'-reject/'e'-edit) for this tool_call: ")
 31|        # 提高容错性，允许用户输入时大小写不敏感，并且去掉首尾空格
 32|        decision_type = decision_type.strip().lower()
 33|
 34|        if decision_type == "approve" or decision_type == "a":
 35|            user_decisions.append({"type": "approve"})
 36|        elif decision_type == "reject" or decision_type == "r":
 37|            user_decisions.append({"type": "reject"})
 38|        elif decision_type == "edit" or decision_type == "e":
 39|            new_args = {}
 40|            for k in action["args"]:
 41|                new_arg_value = input(f"请为{k} = {action['args'][k]}提供一个新值:")
 42|                new_args[k] = new_arg_value
 43|            user_decisions.append({"type": "edit", "edited_action": {"name": action["name"], "args": new_args}})
 44|        else:
 45|            print("无效的决策输入，默认做出的决定为reject")
 46|            user_decisions.append({"type": "reject"})
 47|
 48|    # 验证decisions数量与action_requests数量是否匹配
 49|    if len(user_decisions) != len(action_requests):
 50|        raise ValueError(
 51|            f"决策数量({len(user_decisions)})与待处理工具调用数量({len(action_requests)})不匹配！"
 52|        )
 53|    return user_decisions
 54|
 55|
 56|# 运行给定的LangGraph，支持多轮对话（即每次人类输入引发一次ReAct循环，可以有多次人类对话直至人类输入exit）
 57|def multi_turn_conversation(agent: CompiledStateGraph | AgentTeam | AgentTeam,
 58|                            run_config: RunnableConfig,
 59|                            sandbox: LinuxSandboxBackend | None = None):
 60|    """
 61|    为DeepAgent设计的多轮对话，
 62|    在values模式下流式输出父图、子图消息，流式输出版本version="v2"
 63|    支持父图、子图人在循环发生时收集人类反馈。
 64|    
 65|    Args:
 66|        agent: 编译后的 LangGraph
 67|        run_config: 运行配置
 68|        sandbox: 可选的沙盒实例，用于在退出时清理资源
 69|    """
 70|    if isinstance(agent, AgentTeam) and not sandbox:
 71|        raise ValueError("若提供了AgentTeam实例，则务必提供相应的Sandbox实例")
 72|
 73|    printed_msg_ids = set()  # 用于记录已经输出过的消息id，避免重复输出
 74|    try:
 75|        while True:
 76|            # 第一次用户输入；或者上个ReAct循环完整结束后，等待用户新输入新命令。
 77|
 78|            input_text = input("Your input (or type 'exit' to quit): ")
 79|
 80|            if input_text == "exit":
 81|                print("\n" + "=" * 41 + " 👟用户输入了exit，结束多轮对话 " + "=" * 41 + "\n")
 82|                break
 83|
 84|            agent_input = {"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": input_text}]}
 85|
 86|            print("\n" + "=" * 41 + " 💡下面开启全新的一轮ReAct循环 " + "=" * 41 + "\n")
 87|            human_in_the_loop = False  # 每次开始前默认没发生人在循环
 88|            current_loop_ends_totally = False  # 每次开始前默认本次ReAct没有完整结束
 89|
 90|            while not current_loop_ends_totally:
 91|                # 开启新一轮的 或 继续未完整结束的 ReAct循环
 92|                """
 93|                问：如何控制一轮完整的ReAct循环中模型调用与工具调用的最大循环次数？难道任务完不成就一直让model-tool loop没有上限地一直持续下去吗？
 94|                答：
 95|                Deep Agents 是基于 LangGraph 构建的，create_deep_agent 返回一个编译后的 LangGraph 可运行图。
 96|                LangGraph 的 最基本的ReAct 代理实现下，默认有 max_iterations 参数=25控制着step的上限，
 97|                在这种实现下，每一个step对应一次模型调用节点或工具调用节点，注意某一个step可以有多个工具调用节点并行执行！
 98|                
 99|                默认 max_iterations=25，超过此步数上限会抛出 MaxIterationsExceededError 异常，而不是无限循环。
100|                可以人为在invoke智能体时通过config指定：
101|                result = agent.invoke(
102|                    {"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "query"}]},
103|                    config={
104|                        "recursion_limit": 10  # 最大 10 次 model-tool 循环
105|                    }
106|                )
107|                """
108|                for stream_part in agent.stream(input=agent_input, config=run_config, stream_mode=["values"],
109|                                                subgraphs=True, version="v2"):
110|                    # values流式输出模式下，streamPart对象是ValuesStreamPart类型,包含type、ns、data、interrupts三个key
111|                    stream_part = cast(StreamPart, stream_part)
112|                    # 先判断本次ReAct循环是否需要人类参与
113|                    if stream_part['interrupts']:
114|                        # 如果真需要人类参与
115|                        print(
116|                            "\n" + "=" * 41 + " 🕵️‍♀️人在循环：工具触发中断，需要人类干预，之后才能继续进行本轮ReAct循环 " + "=" * 41 + "\n")
117|                        interrupt = stream_part['interrupts'][0]
118|                        decisions = get_human_decisions(interrupt)
119|
120|                        # Resume execution with decisions
121|                        agent_input = Command(resume={"decisions": decisions})
122|                        human_in_the_loop = True
123|                        # 暂停当前ReAct执行，直接继续下一次ReAct
124|                        break
125|
126|                    namespace = stream_part[
127|                        "ns"]  # namespace` is a tuple with the path to the node where a subgraph is invoked,
128|                    #                               e.g. `("parent_node:<task_id>", "child_node:<task_id>")`.
129|                    data = stream_part["data"]
130|
131|                    messages = data["messages"]
132|
133|                    # 遍历所有消息，而不是只取最后一条
134|                    # 这样可以确保不会漏掉任何新产生的消息（特别是并发工具调用后的多个Tool Message）
135|                    for msg in messages:
136|                        if msg.id not in printed_msg_ids:
137|                            # 只打印之前没打印过的新消息
138|                            if namespace:
139|                                # 如果namespace不是空元组，而是通往哪个调用子图的节点的路径，则说明是data是子图发射的事件（新消息），
140|                                # 比如是 'tools:5927c259-58a6-e7bb-c9fe-0c051e90c825'
141|                                print(
142|                                    "\n" + "=" * 33 + f"👇 下面这条消息是子图的新消息， ns={namespace} \n")
143|                            msg.pretty_print()
144|                            printed_msg_ids.add(msg.id)
145|                # 判断本次ReAct循环是否完整地结束了
146|                if human_in_the_loop:
147|                    # 不是完整地结束
148|                    print("\n" + "=" * 40 + " 👌发生了人在循环，已经收集到人类干预，继续进行本轮ReAct循环\n")
149|                    human_in_the_loop = False  # 继续进行本轮ReAct循环前，恢复默认值
150|                    continue
151|                else:
152|                    # 说明是完整地结束
153|                    print("\n", "=" * 41, "本轮ReAct循环圆满结束", "=" * 41, "\n")
154|                    current_loop_ends_totally = True
155|
156|        print("\n" + "=" * 41 + "✋ 多轮对话成功结束" + "=" * 41 + "\n")
157|    except Exception as e:
158|        logger.error("\n" + "=" * 41 + " ❌多轮对话异常结束" + "=" * 41 + "\n")
159|        logger.error(e, exc_info=True)
160|    finally:
161|        # 确保沙盒资源被正确清理，如果有的话
162|        if sandbox is not None:
163|            logger.info("多轮对话结束，已明确传入一个沙盒实例，开始删除其中维持的会话")
164|            sandbox.delete_session()
165|        elif isinstance(agent,
166|                        (AgentTeam, AgentTeam)) and agent.sandbox is not None:
167|            logger.info("多轮对话结束，开始删除AgentBayBasedTeam实例中的sandbox中的会话")
168|            agent.sandbox.delete_session()
169|
170|
171|# 运行给定的LangGraph，支持多轮对话（即每次人类输入引发一次ReAct循环，可以有多次人类对话直至人类输入exit）
172|async def async_multi_turn_conversation(
173|        agent: CompiledStateGraph | AgentTeam,
174|        run_config: RunnableConfig,
175|        sandbox: LinuxSandboxBackend | None = None):
176|    """
177|    为DeepAgent设计的多轮对话，
178|    在values模式下流式输出父图、子图消息，流式输出版本version="v2"
179|    支持父图、子图人在循环发生时收集人类反馈。
180|
181|    Args:
182|        agent: 编译后的 LangGraph
183|        run_config: 运行配置
184|        sandbox: 可选的沙盒实例，用于在退出时清理资源
185|    """
186|    if not isinstance(agent, (AgentTeam, AgentTeam)) and not sandbox:
187|        raise ValueError("若提供了Agent实例（CompiledStateGraph），则务必提供相应的Sandbox实例")
188|
189|    printed_msg_ids = set()
190|    try:
191|        while True:
192|            # 第一次用户输入；或者上个ReAct循环完整结束后，等待用户新输入新命令。
193|
194|            input_text = input("Your input (or type 'exit' to quit): ")
195|            logger.info(f"多轮对话本次用户输入：{input_text}")
196|
197|            if input_text == "exit":
198|                print("\n" + "=" * 41 + " 👟用户输入了exit，结束多轮对话 " + "=" * 41 + "\n")
199|                break
200|
201|            agent_input = {"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": input_text}]}
202|
203|            print("\n" + "=" * 41 + " 💡下面开启全新的一轮ReAct循环 " + "=" * 41 + "\n")
204|            human_in_the_loop = False  # 每次开始前默认没发生人在循环
205|            current_loop_ends_totally = False  # 每次开始前默认本次ReAct没有完整结束
206|
207|            while not current_loop_ends_totally:
208|                # 开启新一轮的 或 继续未完整结束的 ReAct循环
209|                """
210|                问：如何控制一轮完整的ReAct循环中模型调用与工具调用的最大循环次数？难道任务完不成就一直让model-tool loop没有上限地一直持续下去吗？
211|                答：
212|                Deep Agents 是基于 LangGraph 构建的，create_deep_agent 返回一个编译后的 LangGraph 可运行图。
213|                LangGraph 的 最基本的ReAct 代理实现下，默认有 max_iterations 参数=25控制着step的上限，
214|                在这种实现下，每一个step对应一次模型调用节点或工具调用节点，注意某一个step可以有多个工具调用节点并行执行！
215|                
216|                默认 max_iterations=25，超过此步数上限会抛出 MaxIterationsExceededError 异常，而不是无限循环。
217|                可以人为在invoke智能体时通过config指定：
218|                result = agent.invoke(
219|                    {"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "query"}]},
220|                    config={
221|                        "recursion_limit": 10  # 最大 10 次 model-tool 循环
222|                    }
223|                )
224|                """
225|                async for stream_part in agent.astream(input=agent_input, config=run_config, stream_mode=["values"],
226|                                                       subgraphs=True, version="v2"):
227|                    # values流式输出模式下，streamPart对象是ValuesStreamPart类型,包含type、ns、data、interrupts三个key
228|                    stream_part = cast(StreamPart, stream_part)
229|                    # 先判断本次ReAct循环是否需要人类参与
230|                    if stream_part['interrupts']:
231|                        # 如果真需要人类参与
232|                        print(
233|                            "\n" + "=" * 41 + " 🕵️‍♀️人在循环：工具触发中断，需要人类干预，之后才能继续进行本轮ReAct循环 " + "=" * 41 + "\n")
234|                        interrupt = stream_part['interrupts'][0]
235|                        decisions = get_human_decisions(interrupt)
236|
237|                        # Resume execution with decisions
238|                        agent_input = Command(resume={"decisions": decisions})
239|                        human_in_the_loop = True
240|                        # 暂停当前ReAct执行，直接继续下一次ReAct
241|                        break
242|
243|                    namespace = stream_part["ns"]
244|                    data = stream_part["data"]
245|                    messages = data["messages"]
246|
247|                    # 遍历所有消息，而不是只取最后一条
248|                    # 这样可以确保不会漏掉任何新产生的消息（特别是并发工具调用后的多个Tool Message）
249|                    msg = messages[-1]
250|                    if msg.id not in printed_msg_ids:
251|                        printed_msg_ids.add(msg.id)
252|                        # 只打印之前没打印过的新消息
253|                        if namespace:
254|                            # 如果namespace不是空元组，而是通往哪个调用子图的节点的路径，则说明是data是子图发射的事件（新消息），
255|                            # 比如是 直接调用的子图 'tools:UUID'，表示这个检查点属于名为 "tools" 的节点所调用的子图
256|                            # 比如是 多层嵌套的子图 "outer_node:uuid|inner_node:uuid"，
257|                            # "tools|ns_for_fruit_agent"表示：父图的 tools 节点 → 调用了一个子图，该子图中又有一个名为 ns_for_fruit_agent 的子图
258|                            print("\n" + "=" * 33 + "👇 下面这条消息是子图的新消息\n")
259|                        msg.pretty_print()
260|                # 判断本次ReAct循环是否完整地结束了
261|                if human_in_the_loop:
262|                    # 不是完整地结束
263|                    print("\n" + "=" * 40 + " 👌发生了人在循环，已经收集到人类干预，继续进行本轮ReAct循环\n")
264|                    human_in_the_loop = False  # 继续进行本轮ReAct循环前，恢复默认值
265|                    continue
266|                else:
267|                    # 说明是完整地结束
268|                    print("\n", "=" * 41, "本轮ReAct循环圆满结束", "=" * 41, "\n")
269|                    current_loop_ends_totally = True
270|
271|        print("\n" + "=" * 41 + "✋ 多轮对话成功结束" + "=" * 41 + "\n")
272|    except Exception as e:
273|        logger.error("\n" + "=" * 41 + " ❌多轮对话异常结束" + "=" * 41 + "\n")
274|        logger.error(e, exc_info=True)
275|    finally:
276|        # 确保沙盒资源被正确清理，如果有的话
277|        if sandbox is not None:
278|            logger.info("多轮对话结束，已明确传入一个沙盒实例，开始删除其中维持的会话")
279|            sandbox.delete_session()
280|        elif isinstance(agent,
281|                        (AgentTeam, AgentTeam)) and agent.sandbox is not None:
282|            logger.info("多轮对话结束，开始删除AgentBayBasedTeam实例中的sandbox中的会话")
283|            agent.sandbox.delete_session()
```

  5|def get_current_time():
  6|    """
  7|    Returns the current time as a string in the format "YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS".

  9|    import datetime
 10|    return datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")

## 21. src/utils/mcp.py (148行)

```python
  1|import logging
  2|
  3|from langchain_mcp_adapters.client import MultiServerMCPClient
  4|
  5|from src.config.settings import settings
  6|from src.dev_team.team_config import AGENTBAY_MCP_SERVER_NAME
  7|from src.utils.mcp_interceptors import MCPMonitorInterceptor, UnicodeDecodeInterceptor
  8|
  9|logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 10|
 11|
 12|def get_excluded_tools():
 13|    sandbox_tools = {
 14|        "create_sandbox",  # 沙盒层面的工具
 15|        "get_sandbox_url",
 16|        "system_screenshot",
 17|        "kill_sandbox"
 18|    }
 19|
 20|    # 与LangChain文件系统中间件重合的工具
 21|    redundant_tools = {
 22|        "read_file", "write_file", "edit_file", "copy_file", "move_file",
 23|        "create_directory", "search_file", "list_directory", "read_multiple_files",
 24|        "shell",  # 和sandbox backend提供的execute工具重合
 25|        "run_code",
 26|        "start_ChromeByCDP", "stopChrome",  # Chrome DevTools Protocol相关工具，功能太过低级，不如browser use封装的 playright 工具
 27|    }
 28|
 29|    too_large_returns_tools = {
 30|        "browser_take_screenshot", "browser_snapshot",
 31|        "page_use_screenshot", "screenshot",  # 返回大段base64编码后的字符串的工具
 32|    }
 33|
 34|    useless_tools = {
 35|        "get_file_change",
 36|        "get_file_info"
 37|        "get_metrics"
 38|    }
 39|
 40|    dangerous_tools = {
 41|        "delete_file",  # 危险工具
 42|    }
 43|
 44|    my_excluded_tools = sandbox_tools | redundant_tools | too_large_returns_tools | useless_tools | dangerous_tools
 45|    return my_excluded_tools
 46|
 47|
 48|async def load_wuying_mcp_tools(image_id: str,
 49|                                mcp_tool_name_prefix: bool = True,
 50|                                excluded_tools: set[str] | None = None,
 51|                                included_tools: set[str] | None = None,
 52|                                enable_monitor: bool = False,  # 默认不启用监控拦截器，避免过多日志输出
 53|                                monitor_tool_names: set[str] | None = None,
 54|                                monitor_log_level: str = "INFO",
 55|                                decode_unicode: bool = True,  # 默认启用Unicode解码拦截器，将返回结果中的Unicode编码解码为LLM可读文本
 56|                                unicode_decode_tool_names: set[str] | None = None,
 57|                                ) -> list:
 58|    """异步加载 Wuying MCP 服务器提供的工具。
 59|
 60|    Args:
 61|        image_id: 镜像ID
 62|        mcp_tool_name_prefix: 是否为MCP工具名称添加前缀
 63|        excluded_tools: 要排除的工具名称集合
 64|        included_tools: 要包含的工具名称集合（如果指定，则只保留这些工具）
 65|        enable_monitor: 是否启用MCP工具执行监控拦截器
 66|        monitor_tool_names: 需要监控的工具名称集合。如果为None且enable_monitor=True，则监控所有工具
 67|        monitor_log_level: 监控日志级别，默认为"INFO"
 68|        decode_unicode: 是否启用Unicode解码拦截器，将返回结果中的Unicode转义字符解码为可读文本
 69|        unicode_decode_tool_names: 需要应用Unicode解码的工具名称集合。如果为None且decode_unicode=True，则应用于所有工具
 70|
 71|
 72|    Returns:
 73|        list: 过滤后的 MCP 工具列表
 74|    """
 75|    mcp_image_id = image_id
 76|    logger.info(f"开始加载 Wuying MCP 服务器工具，加载自IMAGEID= {image_id}")
 77|
 78|    try:
 79|        # 创建拦截器列表
 80|        tool_interceptors = []
 81|
 82|        # 1. 添加监控拦截器（如果需要）
 83|        if enable_monitor:
 84|            monitor_interceptor = MCPMonitorInterceptor(
 85|                tool_names=monitor_tool_names,
 86|                log_level=monitor_log_level
 87|            )
 88|            tool_interceptors.append(monitor_interceptor)
 89|            logger.info(f"已启用MCP工具执行监控拦截器 (日志级别: {monitor_log_level})")
 90|
 91|        # 2. 添加Unicode解码拦截器（如果需要）
 92|        if decode_unicode:
 93|            interceptor = UnicodeDecodeInterceptor()
 94|            tool_interceptors.append(interceptor)
 95|            logger.info("已启用Unicode解码拦截器")
 96|
 97|        client = MultiServerMCPClient(
 98|            {
 99|                AGENTBAY_MCP_SERVER_NAME: {
100|                    "transport": "http",
101|                    "url": f"https://agentbay.wuying.aliyuncs.com/v2/mcp?APIKEY={settings.WUYING_AGENTBAY_API_KEY}&IMAGEID={mcp_image_id}",
102|                }
103|            },
104|            tool_interceptors=tool_interceptors if tool_interceptors else None,  # 添加拦截器
105|            tool_name_prefix=mcp_tool_name_prefix,  # 启用MCP工具名前缀，会将'MCP服务器名称_'作为MCP工具名前缀
106|        )
107|        tools = await client.get_tools()
108|
109|        logger.info(
110|            f"从 MCP 服务器获取到 {len(tools)} 个原始工具")
111|        logger.debug(f"原始工具名称: {[tool.name for tool in tools]}")
112|
113|        # 应用工具过滤
113|
114|        # 1. 如果指定了 included_tools，只保留这些工具
115|        if included_tools:
116|            tools = [
117|                tool for tool in tools
118|                if tool.name in included_tools
119|            ]
120|            logger.info(f"按包含列表过滤后剩余 {len(tools)} 个工具")
121|
122|        # 2. 如果指定了 excluded_tools，排除这些工具
123|        if excluded_tools:
124|            original_count = len(tools)
125|            tool_names_to_be_excluded = [
126|                tool.name for tool in tools
127|                if tool.name in excluded_tools
128|            ]
129|            logger.info(f"将排除以下MCP工具：\n{tool_names_to_be_excluded}")
130|
131|            # 保留不需要排除的MCP工具
132|            tools = [
133|                tool for tool in tools
134|                if tool.name not in tool_names_to_be_excluded
135|            ]
136|
137|            excluded_count = original_count - len(tools)
138|            if excluded_count > 0:
139|                logger.info(f"排除了 {excluded_count} 个工具，剩余 {len(tools)} 个工具")
140|
141|        logger.info(
142|            f"✅ 成功加载 {len(tools)} 个 Wuying MCP 工具（已过滤），本次加载的MCP工具由 AgentBay Image（ID={mcp_image_id}）提供")
143|        logger.debug(f"最终工具名称: {[tool.name for tool in tools]}")
144|        return tools
145|    except Exception as e:
146|        logger.error(f"❌ 加载 Wuying MCP 工具失败: {e}", exc_info=True)
147|        logger.warning("将不使用 Wuying MCP 工具，继续初始化")
148|        return []
```

## 22. src/utils/roles_prompt_manager.py (112行)

```python
  1|"""
  2|系统提示词管理器模块
  3|
  4|提供懒加载、缓存机制来管理角色提示词文件。
  5|"""
  6|
  7|import logging
  8|from functools import lru_cache
  9|from pathlib import Path
 10|from typing import Dict
 11|
 12|from src.paths import PROMPTS_DIR
 13|
 14|logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
 15|
 16|# 全局提示词目录（用于缓存键）
 17|_PROMPTS_DIR = PROMPTS_DIR
 18|
 19|# 确保目录存在（模块加载时执行一次）
 20|_PROMPTS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 21|
 22|
 23|def list_available_roles(prompts_dir: Path = _PROMPTS_DIR) -> list:
 24|    """
 25|    扫描指定目录下的所有 md 文件，列出所有可用的角色
 26|
 27|    Args:
 28|        prompts_dir: 提示词文件目录
 29|
 30|    Returns:
 31|        角色名称列表
 32|    """
 33|    if not prompts_dir.exists():
 34|        return []
 35|
 36|    return [
 37|        file.stem
 38|        for file in prompts_dir.glob("*.md")
 39|        if file.is_file()
 40|    ]
 41|
 42|
 43|@lru_cache(maxsize=None)
 44|def get_system_prompt(role: str, prompts_dir: Path = _PROMPTS_DIR) -> str:
 45|    """
 46|    懒加载获取指定角色的系统提示词
 47|
 48|    Args:
 49|        role: 角色、身份 (team_leader, product_manager, architect,
 50|                   project_manager, engineer, qa_engineer)
 51|        prompts_dir: 提示词文件目录
 52|
 53|    Returns:
 54|        角色对应的系统提示词内容
 55|
 56|    Raises:
 57|        FileNotFoundError: 当提示词文件不存在时
 58|        UnicodeDecodeError: 当文件编码错误时
 59|    """
 60|    prompt_file = prompts_dir / f"{role}.md"
 61|
 62|    if not prompt_file.exists():
 63|        available_roles = list_available_roles(prompts_dir)
 64|        raise FileNotFoundError(
 65|            f"Role prompt file not found: {prompt_file}\n"
 66|            f"Available roles: {available_roles}"
 67|        )
 68|
 69|    try:
 70|        with open(prompt_file, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
 71|            return f.read()
 72|    except UnicodeDecodeError as e:
 73|        logger.error(f"Failed to read prompt file {prompt_file}: {e}")
 74|        raise
 75|
 76|
 77|# 获取某些角色系统提示词
 78|@lru_cache(maxsize=None)
 79|def get_system_prompts_for_roles(role_names: tuple, prompts_dir: Path = _PROMPTS_DIR) -> Dict[str, str]:
 80|    """
 81|    获取指定角色的系统提示词
 82|
 83|    Args:
 84|        role_names: 角色名称列表
 85|        prompts_dir: 提示词文件目录
 86|
 87|    Returns:
 88|        包含指定角色提示词的字典
 89|    """
 90|    return {
 91|        role_name: get_system_prompt(role_name, prompts_dir)
 92|        for role_name in role_names
 93|    }
 94|
 95|
 96|@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
 97|def get_all_system_prompts(prompts_dir: Path = _PROMPTS_DIR) -> Dict[str, str]:
 98|    """
 99|    获取所有角色的系统提示词（带缓存）
100|
101|    Args:
102|        prompts_dir: 提示词文件目录
103|
104|    Returns:
105|        包含所有角色提示词的字典
106|    """
107|    roles = list_available_roles(prompts_dir)
108|
109|    return {
110|        role: get_system_prompt(role, prompts_dir)
111|        for role in roles
112|    }
```

060|
061|try:
062|    raw_prefix.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')

065|    print(json.dumps({{

068|        'content': base64.b64encode(raw_prefix).decode('ascii')
069|    }}))
070|    sys.exit(0)
071|
072|offset = {offset}
073|limit = {limit}
074|line_count = 0
075|returned_lines = 0
076|truncated = False
077|parts = []
078|current_bytes = 0

117|
118|
119|# 该类通过无影agentbay的session实现sandbox backend
120|# 目前仅仅支持linux session，因为agentbay仅linux_latest镜像预装了python命令
121|# BaseSandbox通过调用self.execute方法执行python3 -c "python脚本"来提供文件系统接口
122|class LinuxSandboxBackend(BaseSandbox):
123|    """基于无影AgentBay实现的云沙盒后端。 注意！沙盒会话最长运行时间需要在镜像中配置，的确为linux_latest配置了2h最长会话运行时间。
124|
125|    该类通过无影AgentBay的session提供真正的云端沙盒环境，
126|    所有文件操作和命令执行都在云端进行，而非本地。
127|    """
128|
129|    def __init__(self, *,
130|                 task_name: str = "",
131|                 task_context_box_name: str = "",
132|                 local_dir_for_task_products: Path,
133|                 image_id: str = AGENTBAY_IMAGE_ID,
134|                 api_key: str = settings.WUYING_AGENTBAY_API_KEY, idle_timeout: int = 30 * 60,
135|                 ):
136|        """初始化LinuxSandboxBackend的静态资源。
137|
138|        Args:
139|            task_name: 当前软件开发任务的名称，用于创建持久化的存储盒，持久化保存智能体的文件写入操作（允许智能体可以访问该沙盒）。因此可以后续下载当前软件的成果物文件到本地
140|            api_key: AgentBay API密钥
141|            image_id: 沙盒镜像ID
142|            idle_timeout: 云端会话空闲自动释放时间（秒），默认1h（60*60秒），太短的话，很有可能在等待智能体输出大块内容（这需要llm耗费10min左右来输出3.8万个字符）之前，该会话就会被自动释放了！！！
143|            :param local_dir_for_task_products:
144|        """
145|        self.task_name = task_name
146|
147|        if not task_context_box_name:
148|            raise ValueError("创建Sandbox 实例必须传入一个任务上下文盒子名称")
149|
150|        self.task_context_box_name = task_context_box_name
151|        self.task_context_box_id = None  # 存本次开发时所用的任务上下文盒子的ID
152|
153|        self.local_dir_for_task_products = local_dir_for_task_products
154|        self.agentbay = AgentBay(api_key=api_key)
155|        self.context_service = self.agentbay.context
156|        self.image_id = image_id
157|        self.sandbox_type = "linux"
158|
159|        self._idle_timeout = idle_timeout
160|        self._sandbox_max_running_time_str = "3 hours"  # 当前镜像的最长持续运行时间（字符串描述）
161|        self._sandbox_max_running_time_seconds = 3 * 60 * 60  # 3小时转换为秒
162|        self.time_for_products_download = 10  # 在达到当前镜像的最长持续运行时间的10 分钟前主动提前释放沙盒会话
163|
164|        # 确定当前 task的context box 在sandbox中的挂载路径
165|        self._task_context_box_mount_path_prefix = CONTEXT_MOUNT_PATH_PREFIX_OF_NEW_TASK_CONTEXT_BOX[
166|            f'{self.sandbox_type}']
167|        self._task_context_box_leaf_dir_name = to_snake_case(task_name) if task_name else ""
168|        self.task_context_box_mount_path = f"{self._task_context_box_mount_path_prefix}/{self._task_context_box_leaf_dir_name}"
169|        logger.info(
170|            f"当前Task Context Box 的云端挂载路径为{self.task_context_box_mount_path}\n"
171|            f"智能体必须在该路径下进行开发、文件写入，否则任务成果物无法下载到本地！"
172|        )
173|
174|        my_context_syncs = self._get_context_syncs()
175|
176|        for i, ctx_sync in enumerate(my_context_syncs):
177|            logger.debug(f"    [{i}] 存储盒 ID: {ctx_sync.context_id}")
178|
179|        params = CreateSessionParams(
180|            image_id=self.image_id,
181|            context_syncs=my_context_syncs,
182|            idle_release_timeout=self._idle_timeout
183|        )
184|
185|        # 开始创建会话
186|        try:
187|            max_retries = 25
188|            retry_delay = 5  # 初始重试延迟5秒
189|            for attempt in range(max_retries):
190|                # 创建会话，该会话具备持久化数据
191|                result: SessionResult = self.agentbay.create(params)
192|
193|                if result.success:
194|                    # 打印会话成功创建的信息
195|                    self.session = result.session
196|                    self._session_id = self.session.session_id
197|                    self.sandbox_id = self._session_id
198|
199|                    # 记录会话创建时间
200|                    self._session_start_time = datetime.now()
201|                    logger.info(f"\n"
202|                                f"✅ AgentBay 会话创建成功, SessionId={self._session_id}\n"
203|                                f"🕒 会话创建时间: {self._session_start_time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
204|                    break
205|                else:
206|                    # 判断是否可重试
207|                    error_msg = getattr(result, 'error_message', '未知错误')
208|                    # 检查是否是资源不足的错误
209|                    if "All candidate AppInstanceGroups have no idle AppInstances now, please retry later" in error_msg:
210|                        if attempt < max_retries - 1:  # 不是最后一次尝试
211|                            logger.warning(
212|                                f"⚠️ AgentBay 资源不足，第 {attempt + 1} 次重试，等待 {retry_delay} 秒后重试...\n"
213|                                f"错误详情:\n{error_msg}"
214|                            )
215|                            time.sleep(retry_delay)
216|                            retry_delay *= 2  # 指数退避策略
217|                            continue
218|                        else:
219|                            logger.error(f"❌ AgentBay 资源不足，已达到最大重试次数 {max_retries}，放弃重试")
220|                            logger.error(f"   最终错误: {error_msg}")
221|                            raise  # 重新抛出异常
222|                    else:
223|                        # 非资源不足的错误，直接抛出
224|                        raise RuntimeError(f"创建会话时发生未预期的异常: {error_msg}")
225|        except Exception as e:
226|            # 其他类型的异常，直接抛出
227|            logger.error(f"❌ 创建会话时发生异常: {e}", exc_info=True)
228|            raise
229|
230|        logger.info(f"{self.__class__.__name__}  实例初始化完成")
231|
232|    def get_existing_task_context(self):
233|        """获取或创建当前任务的 Context。

239|            return None

242|
243|        return res.context
244|
245|    def _get_task_context_sync(self) -> ContextSync:
246|        """获取当前任务的 Context同步配置。"""
247|        task_context_1 = self.get_existing_task_context()
248|        self.task_context_box_id = task_context_1.id
249|        return ContextSync.new(

251|            path=self.task_context_box_mount_path
252|        )
253|
254|    def _get_context_syncs(self) -> list[ContextSync]:
255|        """获取所有需要挂载的 Context 同步配置。
256|
257|        Returns:
258|            list[ContextSync]: 包含 agent 资源 context 和（可选的）task context
259|        """
260|        my_context_syncs = []
261|
262|        # 1. 添加任务 context（如果存在 task_name）

264|        if task_sync is not None:

267|        return my_context_syncs
268|
269|    @property
270|    def id(self) -> str:
271|        """Unique identifier for the sandbox backend."""
272|        if self.session is None:
273|            raise RuntimeError("Session not initialized")
274|        return self.session.session_id
275|
276|    def check_session_remaining_time(self):
277|        """检查会话剩余时间，如果接近最大运行时间则提前清理并抛出异常。
278|
279|        ⚠️ 在通过当前对象的_session属性调用AgentBay MCP 工具前必须调用此方法，以防止会话过期导致操作失败。
280|
281|        Raises:
282|            RuntimeError: 当会话剩余时间不足10分钟时抛出
283|        """
284|        if self._session_start_time is None:
285|            logger.warning("⚠️ 会话创建时间未记录，跳过时间检查")
286|            return
287|
288|        # 计算已运行时间
289|        elapsed_time = datetime.now() - self._session_start_time
290|        elapsed_seconds = elapsed_time.total_seconds()
291|
292|        # 计算剩余时间
293|        remaining_seconds = self._sandbox_max_running_time_seconds - elapsed_seconds
294|
295|        if remaining_seconds < 0:
296|            # 会话已过期，但是还有工具调用在执行，则抛出异常给ToolRetryMiddleware的钩子函数
297|            raise RuntimeError(f"⚠️ 沙盒会话已被主动提前断开，并且距离断开已经过了一段时间！\n"
298|                               f"   - 最大运行时间: {self._sandbox_max_running_time_str} ({self._sandbox_max_running_time_seconds}秒)\n"
299|                               f"   - 已运行时间: {elapsed_seconds / 60:.1f} 分钟\n"
300|                               f"   - 会话已经过期了{(-remaining_seconds) / 60} 分钟 {(-remaining_seconds) % 60} 秒\n")
301|
302|        # 如果剩余时间不足10分钟=10*60秒，提前清理并抛出异常
303|        time_threshold_seconds = self.time_for_products_download * 60
304|        if 0 <= remaining_seconds <= time_threshold_seconds:
305|            remaining_minutes = remaining_seconds / 60
306|
307|            logger.debug(f"🕒 会话运行状态: 已运行 {elapsed_seconds / 60:.1f} 分钟, 剩余 {remaining_minutes:.1f} 分钟")
308|            if self.session is not None:
309|                # 第一次检测到应该释放，那就释放。
310|                error_msg = (
311|                    f"沙盒会话即将达到最大运行时间限制！\n"
312|                    f"   - 最大运行时间: {self._sandbox_max_running_time_str} ({self._sandbox_max_running_time_seconds}秒)\n"
313|                    f"   - 已运行时间: {elapsed_seconds / 60:.1f} 分钟\n"
314|                    f"   - 剩余时间: {remaining_minutes:.1f} 分钟\n"
315|                    f"   - 会话ID: {self._session_id}\n"
316|                    f"为避免会话突然过期导致沙盒软件成果物无法及时下载到本地，将主动提前断开当前沙盒会话。"
317|                )
318|
319|                logger.error(f"❌ {error_msg}")
320|
321|                # 主动删除会话，同步数据到 OSS
322|                try:
323|                    self.download_task_artifacts(self.local_dir_for_task_products)
324|                    self.delete_session()
325|                    logger.info("✅ 为避免会话突然过期导致沙盒软件成果物无法及时下载到本地，已主动清理成功即将过期的会话")
326|                except Exception as e:
327|                    logger.warning(
328|                        f"⚠️ 为避免会话突然过期导致沙盒软件成果物无法及时下载到本地，主动清理会话时出现异常: {e}")
329|
330|                else:
331|                    raise RuntimeError(error_msg)
332|            elif self._session_id is None:
333|                # 已经释放掉，但是还有工具调用正在执行，则抛出异常给ToolRetryMiddleware的钩子函数
334|                raise RuntimeError(f"⚠️ 沙盒会话已被主动提前断开！\n"
335|                                   f"   - 最大运行时间: {self._sandbox_max_running_time_str} ({self._sandbox_max_running_time_seconds}秒)\n"
336|                                   f"   - 已运行时间: {elapsed_seconds / 60:.1f} 分钟\n")
337|
338|    def execute(
339|            self,
340|            command: str,
341|            *,
342|            timeout: int | None = None,
343|    ) -> ExecuteResponse:
344|        """Execute a command in the sandbox and return ExecuteResponse.
345|
346|        确保云端 Python 脚本使用 UTF-8 编码执行
347|        """
348|        # 在下载文件前检查会话剩余时间
349|        self.check_session_remaining_time()
350|
351|        # 如果智能体调用execute工具时没有传入timeout参数，那么默认timeout是50秒
352|        if timeout is None:
353|            timeout = 50
354|
355|        # Convert timeout from seconds (protocol) to milliseconds (AgentBay)
356|        timeout_ms = int(timeout * 1000)
357|
358|        # 为 Python 命令添加 UTF-8 编码环境变量,以便智能体调用read、ls、grep、glob工具时看到正确的文字输出。
359|        # 检测是否是 Python 命令(已知 BaseSandbox.read/ls/grep/glob 等方法执行时,底层会调用execute方法执行python代码,方式大致为python -c "python代码")
360|        # 关键修复：只在 python/python3 命令前添加环境变量，而不是在整个命令前添加
361|        if 'python' in command.lower():
362|
363|            if self.sandbox_type == "linux":
364|                # Linux/Unix: 使用正则表达式找到所有 python/python3 命令并在其前添加 PYTHONUTF8=1
365|                # 策略：将命令按 && || ; | 分割，对每个子命令检查是否以 python 开头
366|                modified_command = process_command_parts(command)
367|
368|                if modified_command != command:
369|                    logger.debug(f"🔧 已为 Python 命令添加 UTF-8 编码环境变量")
370|                    logger.debug(f"   原始命令: {command}")
371|                    logger.debug(f"   修改后: {modified_command}")
372|                    command = modified_command
373|
374|            elif self.sandbox_type == "windows":
375|                # Windows CMD: 需要在每个 python 命令前添加 set PYTHONUTF8=1 &&
376|                # 但 Windows 下处理更复杂，简单起见，如果命令以 python 开头则添加
377|                if command.strip().lower().startswith('python'):
378|                    command = f"set PYTHONUTF8=1 && {command}"
379|                    logger.debug(f"🔧 已为 Python 命令添加 UTF-8 编码环境变量 (Windows)")
380|
381|        # agentbay execute工具默认的超时时间是50秒
382|        command_result: CommandResult = self.session.command.execute_command(command, timeout_ms=timeout_ms)
383|
384|        output = command_result.output  # shell命令执行后的stdout+stderr
385|        exit_code = command_result.exit_code
386|
387|        if not command_result.success:
388|            error_msg = command_result.error_message
389|            output = error_msg
390|            if r'"system_error":"Command timed out"' in error_msg:
391|                output = f"shell命令执行超时（timeout={timeout}），请为本次shell命令（{command}）设置更大的timeout后重试！"
392|
393|            logger.error(
394|                f"❌ session.command.execute_command 执行失败:\n"
395|                f"失败的shell命令：\n"
396|                f"{command}\n"
397|                f"超时时间设置：{timeout} 秒\n"
398|                f"智能体所见的shell工具执行结果：\n"
399|                f"{output}\n"
400|                f"API返回的错误消息：\n"
401|                f"{error_msg}\n")
402|            return ExecuteResponse(
403|                output=output,
404|                exit_code=exit_code,
405|                truncated=False
406|            )
407|
408|        logger.debug(f"✅ session.command.execute_command 执行成功\n"
409|                     f"执行的命令：\n"
410|                     f"{command}\n"
411|                     f"超时时间设置：{timeout} 秒\n"
412|                     f"命令执行结果：\n"
413|                     f"{output}")
414|
415|        return ExecuteResponse(
416|            output=output,
417|            exit_code=exit_code,
418|            truncated=False
419|        )
420|
421|    def upload_files(self, files: list[tuple[str, bytes]]) -> list[FileUploadResponse]:
422|        """Upload multiple files to the sandbox.
423|        
424|        使用 AgentBay 原生的 upload_file() API，而不是 write_file()
425|        通过临时文件桥接 bytes → 本地文件 → 云端
426|        """
427|        # 在下载文件前检查会话剩余时间
428|        self.check_session_remaining_time()
429|
430|        responses = []
431|        for file_path, content in files:
432|            temp_file_path = None
433|            logger.debug(f"📤 正在上传文件: {file_path} ({len(content)} 字节)")
434|
435|            # 确保父目录存在
436|            parent_dir = str(Path(file_path).parent)
437|            if parent_dir and parent_dir != '.':
438|                self.session.file_system.create_directory(parent_dir)
439|
440|            # 创建临时文件，使用 AgentBay 原生 upload_file API
441|            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode='wb', delete=False, suffix='.tmp') as temp_file:
442|                temp_file.write(content)
443|                temp_file_path = temp_file.name
444|
445|            # 使用 AgentBay 原生 upload_file API（支持二进制文件）
446|            upload_result: UploadResult = self.session.file_system.upload_file(
447|                local_path=temp_file_path,
448|                remote_path=file_path
449|            )
450|
451|            if upload_result.success:
452|                logger.debug(f"✅ 上传成功: {file_path}")
453|                responses.append(FileUploadResponse(path=file_path))
454|            else:
455|                error_msg = getattr(upload_result, 'error_message', '未知错误')
456|                logger.error(
457|                    f"❌ session.file_system.upload_file 执行失败:\n目标文件路径 {file_path},\n错误: {error_msg}")
458|                # 清理临时文件
459|                if temp_file_path and os.path.exists(temp_file_path):
460|                    try:
461|                        os.unlink(temp_file_path)
462|                    except Exception as e:
463|                        logger.warning(f"⚠️ Failed to clean up temp file: {temp_file_path}, Error: {e}")
464|
465|                # 将 AgentBay 错误映射到标准 FileOperationError
466|                standardized_error = map_agentbay_error_to_file_operation_error(error_msg)
467|                if not isinstance(standardized_error, FileOperationError):
468|                    standardized_error = error_msg
469|                responses.append(FileUploadResponse(
470|                    path=file_path,
471|                    error=standardized_error
472|                ))
473|        return responses
474|
475|    def download_files(self, paths: list[str]) -> list[FileDownloadResponse]:
476|        """Download multiple files from the sandbox.
477|        
478|        优化：使用 AgentBay 原生的 download_file() API，而不是 read_file()
479|        通过临时文件桥接 云端 → 本地文件 → bytes
480|        """
481|        # 在下载文件前检查会话剩余时间
482|        self.check_session_remaining_time()
483|
484|        responses = []
485|        for file_path in paths:
486|            temp_file_path = None
487|            logger.debug(f"📥 正在下载文件: {file_path}")
488|
489|            # 关键优化：创建临时文件，使用 AgentBay 原生 download_file API
490|            with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode='wb', delete=False, suffix='.tmp') as temp_file:
491|                temp_file_path = temp_file.name
492|
493|            # 使用 AgentBay 原生 download_file API（支持二进制文件）
494|            download_result: DownloadResult = self.session.file_system.download_file(
495|                remote_path=file_path,
496|                local_path=temp_file_path
497|            )
498|
499|            if download_result.success:
500|                # 读取临时文件为 bytes
501|                with open(temp_file_path, 'rb') as f:
502|                    content = f.read()
503|
504|                logger.debug(f"✅ 下载成功: {file_path} ({len(content)} 字节)")
505|                responses.append(FileDownloadResponse(
506|                    path=file_path,
507|                    content=content
508|                ))
509|            else:
510|                error_msg = getattr(download_result, 'error_message', '未知错误')
511|                logger.error(
512|                    f"❌ session.file_system.download_file 执行失败:\n目标文件路径 {file_path},\n错误: {error_msg}")
513|                # 清理临时文件
514|                if temp_file_path and os.path.exists(temp_file_path):
515|                    try:
516|                        os.unlink(temp_file_path)
517|                    except Exception as e:
518|                        logger.warning(f"⚠️ Failed to clean up temp file: {temp_file_path}, Error: {e}")
519|
520|                # 将 AgentBay 错误映射到标准 FileOperationError
521|                standardized_error = map_agentbay_error_to_file_operation_error(error_msg)
522|                if not isinstance(standardized_error, FileOperationError):
523|                    standardized_error = error_msg
524|                responses.append(FileDownloadResponse(
525|                    path=file_path,
526|                    error=standardized_error
527|                ))
528|
529|        return responses
530|
531|    # 重写父类文件系统read方法
532|    def read(self, file_path: str, offset: int = 0, limit: int = 2000):
533|        """读取文件内容，添加日志记录并处理 AgentBay 的 base64 编码响应。"""
534|        logger.debug(f"📖 [read] 读取文件: {file_path} (offset={offset}, limit={limit})")
535|
536|        # 获取文件类型
537|        file_type = _get_file_type(file_path)
538|        logger.debug(f"LangChain BaseSandbox 判断该文件的类型为: {file_type}")
539|        path_b64 = base64.b64encode(file_path.encode("utf-8")).decode("ascii")
540|
541|        # 构造命令（与 BaseSandbox.read() 相同）
542|        cmd = READ_COMMAND_TEMPLATE.format(
543|            path_b64=path_b64,
544|            file_type=file_type,
545|            offset=int(offset),
546|            limit=int(limit),
547|        )
548|
549|        # 执行命令
550|        execute_result = self.execute(cmd)
551|        output = execute_result.output.rstrip()  # 根据cmd的内容，output应该时JSON格式字符串
552|
553|        try:
554|            data = json.loads(output)
555|        except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):
556|            error_msg = f"File '{file_path}': unexpected server response: {execute_result}"
557|            logger.error(f"❌ [read] {error_msg}")
558|            return ReadResult(error=error_msg)
559|
560|        if not isinstance(data, dict):
561|            error_msg = f"File '{file_path}': unexpected server response: {execute_result}"
562|            logger.error(f"❌ [read] {error_msg}")
563|            return ReadResult(error=error_msg)
564|
565|        if "error" in data:
566|            error_msg = f"File '{file_path}': {data['error']}"
567|            logger.warning(f"⚠️ [read] 发现 REAM_COMMAND_TEMPLATE 命令打印出的错误信息 {error_msg}")
568|            return ReadResult(error=error_msg)
569|
570|        # 成功读取
571|        content_length = len(data["content"])
572|        encoding = data.get("encoding", "utf-8")
573|        logger.debug(f"✅ [read] 成功读取文件 '{file_path}' ({content_length} 字符, encoding={encoding})")
574|
575|        return ReadResult(
576|            file_data=FileData(
577|                content=data["content"],
578|                encoding=encoding,
579|            )
580|        )
581|
582|    def _download_recursive(self, cloud_dir: str, local_dir: Path) -> int:
583|        """递归下载云端目录到本地（智能过滤中间产物）。
584|
585|        使用 AgentBay 原生的 download_file() API，而不是 read_file()
586|
587|        Args:
588|            cloud_dir: 云端目录路径
589|            local_dir: 本地目标目录
590|
591|        Returns:
592|            下载的文件数量
593|        """
594|
595|        downloaded_count = 0
596|        skipped_count = 0
597|
598|        try:
599|            list_result = self.session.file_system.list_directory(cloud_dir)
600|            if not list_result.success or not list_result.entries:
601|                logger.error(
602|                    f"❌ session.file_system.list_directory调用失败，\n 目标路径：{cloud_dir}\n错误信息:\n {list_result.error_message}")
603|                return 0
604|
605|            # 打印第一个 entry 的所有属性，了解其结构
606|            if list_result.entries:
607|                first_entry = list_result.entries[0]
608|                logger.debug(f"🔍 DirectoryEntry 对象属性: {dir(first_entry)}")
609|                logger.debug(f"   - 类型: {type(first_entry)}")
610|                # 尝试访问常见属性
611|                for attr in ['path', 'name', 'full_path', 'file_path', 'entry_path']:
612|                    if hasattr(first_entry, attr):
613|                        logger.debug(f"   - {attr}: {getattr(first_entry, attr)}")
614|
615|            for entry in list_result.entries:
616|                # 尝试多种可能的属性名
617|                cloud_path = None
618|                for attr_name in ['path', 'full_path', 'file_path', 'entry_path']:
619|                    if hasattr(entry, attr_name):
620|                        cloud_path = getattr(entry, attr_name)
621|                        break
622|
623|                # 如果找不到完整路径属性，但有 name 属性，则手动拼接完整路径
624|                if cloud_path is None and hasattr(entry, 'name'):
625|                    # name 只是文件名，需要与父目录拼接
626|                    cloud_path = f"{cloud_dir}/{entry.name}"
627|                    logger.debug(f"   📝 使用 name 属性拼接路径: {cloud_path}")
628|
629|                if cloud_path is None:
630|                    logger.error(f"❌ 无法找到 entry 的路径属性，可用属性: {dir(entry)}", exc_info=True)
631|                    continue
632|
633|                # 统一使用 POSIX 路径格式（正斜杠）进行相对路径计算
634|                # 云端路径始终是 Linux 格式（正斜杠），不能用 Windows 的 Path 对象处理
635|                base_path = f"{self._task_context_box_mount_path_prefix}/{self._task_context_box_leaf_dir_name}"
636|
637|                # 使用纯字符串操作计算相对路径，避免跨平台路径格式问题
638|                if cloud_path.startswith(base_path + '/'):
639|                    relative_path_str = cloud_path[len(base_path) + 1:]
640|                elif cloud_path == base_path:
641|                    continue  # 跳过基础路径本身
642|                else:
643|                    logger.warning(f"⚠️ 路径不匹配: {cloud_path} 不以 {base_path} 开头")
644|                    continue
645|
646|                # 现在才转换为 Path 对象用于本地文件系统操作
647|                relative_path = Path(relative_path_str)
648|
649|                # 检查是否应该跳过
650|                if should_skip_path(relative_path):
651|                    skipped_count += 1
652|                    continue
653|
654|                local_path = local_dir / relative_path
655|
656|                if entry.is_directory:
657|                    # 递归下载子目录
658|                    local_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
659|                    downloaded_count += self._download_recursive(cloud_path, local_dir)
660|                else:
661|                    # 使用临时文件 + download_file() API
662|                    temp_file_path = None
663|                    try:
664|                        # 创建临时文件
665|                        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(mode='wb', delete=False, suffix='.tmp') as temp_file:
666|                            temp_file_path = temp_file.name
667|
668|                        # 使用原生 download_file API
669|                        download_result = self.session.file_system.download_file(
670|                            remote_path=cloud_path,
671|                            local_path=temp_file_path
672|                        )
673|
674|                        if download_result.success:
675|                            # 读取临时文件并写入目标位置
676|                            with open(temp_file_path, 'rb') as f:
677|                                file_content = f.read()
678|
679|                            # 使用统一的文件写入方法
680|                            write_content_to_file(local_path, file_content)
681|
682|                            downloaded_count += 1
683|                            logger.debug(f"✅ 已下载: {cloud_path} -> {local_path}")
684|                        else:
685|                            error_msg = getattr(download_result, 'error', '未知错误')
686|                            logger.warning(f"⚠️ 下载失败: {cloud_path}, 错误: {error_msg}")
687|
688|                    except Exception as e:
689|                        logger.error(f"❌ 下载异常: {cloud_path}, 错误: {e}", exc_info=True)
690|                    finally:
691|                        # 清理临时文件
692|                        if temp_file_path and os.path.exists(temp_file_path):
693|                            try:
694|                                os.unlink(temp_file_path)
695|                            except Exception as e:
696|                                logger.warning(f"⚠️ 清理临时文件失败: {temp_file_path}, 错误: {e}")
697|
698|        except Exception as e:
699|            logger.info(f"⚠️ 递归下载出错: {e}")
700|
701|        if skipped_count > 0:
702|            logger.info(f"   ℹ️  已跳过 {skipped_count} 个中间产物/缓存文件")
703|
704|        return downloaded_count
705|
706|    def download_task_artifacts(self, local_download_dir: Path):
707|        """下载当前任务的所有成果物到本地目录。
708|        
709|        Args:
710|            local_download_dir: 本地下载目标目录
711|            
712|        Returns:
713|            bool: 是否成功下载
714|        """
715|        logger.info(
716|            f"📥 下载任务成果物, 任务存储盒子名={self.task_context_box_name}, 本地目标目录={local_download_dir}")
717|
718|        if not self.task_context_box_name:
719|            logger.warning("⚠️ 未关联任务存储盒，跳过下载")
720|            return
721|
722|        if not self.session:
723|            logger.error("❌ 会话不存在，跳过下载", exc_info=True)
724|            logger.info("会话不存在，无法下载成果物")
725|            return
726|
727|        try:
728|            cloud_path = self.task_context_box_mount_path
729|            logger.info(f"📂 当前软件开发任务 云端项目空间源路径: {cloud_path}")
730|
731|            # 确保本地目录存在
732|            local_download_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
733|
734|            downloaded_count = self._download_recursive(cloud_path, local_download_dir)
735|
736|            logger.info(f"✅ 下载完成: 共 {downloaded_count} 个文件")
737|            logger.info(f"✅ 成功下载 {downloaded_count} 个文件到: {local_download_dir}")
738|
739|        except Exception as e:
740|            logger.error(f"❌ 下载失败: {e}", exc_info=True)
741|            return
742|
743|    def delete_session(self):
744|        """清理资源，删除AgentBay会话"""
745|        if not self.session:
746|            logger.info("🧹 当前会话为None，无需清理")
747|
748|        if self.session:
749|            logger.info(f"🧹 当前会话不为None，开始手动释放会话:\n {self}")
750|            try:
751|                # 将当前会话中对存储盒内文件的更改同步到OSS上该存储盒对应位置
752|                self.agentbay.delete(self.session, sync_context=True)
753|                logger.info("✅ 会话手动释放成功")
754|            except Exception as e:
755|                logger.warning(f"❌ 手动释放会话过程中捕获异常: {e}")
756|            finally:
757|                self.session = None
758|                self._session_id = None
759|                self.sandbox_id = None
760|
761|    def __str__(self):
762|        return (f"MySandbox(\n"
763|                f"      task_context_box_name={self.task_context_box_name},\n"
764|                f"      task_context_box_id={self.task_context_box_id},\n"
765|                f"      image_id={self.image_id},\n"
766|                f"      session_id={self._session_id},\n"
767|                f"      sandbox_max_running_time={self._sandbox_max_running_time_str},\n"
768|                f"      idle_timeout={self._idle_timeout})")
769|
770|    def __enter__(self):
771|        """支持 with 语句：进入时返回实例"""
772|        return self
773|
774|    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
775|        """支持 with 语句：退出时自动清理资源"""
776|        if self.session:
777|            logger.warning(
778|                f"已经退出{self.__class__.__name__}的with块，但当前会话没有被杀死，由退出函数负责兜底删除该还活跃着的会话")
779|            self.delete_session()
780|        if exc_type:
781|            logger.info(f"❌ {self.__class__.__name__} 运行期间发生错误: {exc_val}", exc_info=True)
782|        return False  # 不吞掉异常
783|
```
---

## 24. src/custom_middleware/my_skills.py (870行)

```python
001|"""Skills middleware for loading and exposing agent skills to the system prompt.
002|
003|This module implements Anthropic's agent skills pattern with progressive disclosure,
004|loading skills from backend storage via configurable sources.
005|
006|## Architecture
007|
008|Skills are loaded from one or more **sources** - paths in a backend where skills are
009|organized. Sources are loaded in order, with later sources overriding earlier ones
010|when skills have the same name (last one wins). This enables layering: base -> user
011|-> project -> team skills.
012|
013|The middleware uses backend APIs exclusively (no direct filesystem access), making it
014|portable across different storage backends (filesystem, state, remote storage, etc.).
015|
016|For StateBackend (ephemeral/in-memory):
017|```python
018|SkillsMiddleware(backend=StateBackend(), ...)
019|```
020|
021|## Skill Structure
022|
023|Each skill is a directory containing a SKILL.md file with YAML frontmatter:
024|
025|```
026|/skills/user/web-research/
027|├── SKILL.md          # Required: YAML frontmatter + markdown instructions
028|└── helper.py         # Optional: supporting files
029|```
030|
031|SKILL.md format:
032|```markdown
033|---
034|name: web-research
035|description: Structured approach to conducting thorough web research
036|license: MIT
037|---
038|
039|# Web Research Skill
040|
041|## When to Use
042|- User asks you to research a topic
043|...
044|```
045|
046|## Skill Metadata (SkillMetadata)
047|
048|Parsed from YAML frontmatter per Agent Skills specification:
049|- `name`: Skill identifier (max 64 chars, lowercase alphanumeric and hyphens)
050|- `description`: What the skill does (max 1024 chars)
051|- `path`: Backend path to the SKILL.md file
052|- Optional: `license`, `compatibility`, `metadata`, `allowed_tools`
053|
054|## Sources
055|
056|Sources are simply paths to skill directories in the backend. The source name is
057|derived from the last component of the path (e.g., "/skills/user/" -> "user").
058|
059|Example sources:
060|```python
061|[
062|    "/skills/user/",
063|    "/skills/project/"
064|]
065|```
066|
067|## Path Conventions
068|
069|All paths use POSIX conventions (forward slashes) via `PurePosixPath`:
070|- Backend paths: "/skills/user/web-research/SKILL.md"
071|- Virtual, platform-independent
072|- Backends handle platform-specific conversions as needed
073|
074|## Usage
075|
076|```python
077|from deepagents.backends.state import StateBackend
078|from deepagents.middleware.skills import SkillsMiddleware
079|
080|middleware = SkillsMiddleware(
081|    backend=my_backend,
082|    sources=[
083|        "/skills/base/",
084|        "/skills/user/",
085|        "/skills/project/",
086|    ],
087|)
088|```
089|"""
090|
091|from __future__ import annotations
092|
093|import logging
094|import re
095|from pathlib import PurePosixPath
096|from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Annotated
097|
098|import yaml
099|from langchain.agents.middleware.types import PrivateStateAttr
100|
101|if TYPE_CHECKING:
102|    from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
103|
104|    from langchain_core.runnables import RunnableConfig
105|    from langgraph.runtime import Runtime
106|
107|    from deepagents.backends.protocol import BACKEND_TYPES, BackendProtocol
108|
109|from typing import NotRequired, TypedDict
110|
111|from langchain.agents.middleware.types import (
112|    AgentMiddleware,
113|    AgentState,
114|    ContextT,
115|    ModelRequest,
116|    ModelResponse,
117|    ResponseT,
118|)
119|from langgraph.prebuilt import ToolRuntime
120|
121|from deepagents.backends.protocol import LsResult
122|from deepagents.middleware._utils import append_to_system_message
123|
124|logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
125|
126|# Security: Maximum size for SKILL.md files to prevent DoS attacks (10MB)
127|MAX_SKILL_FILE_SIZE = 10 * 1024 * 1024
128|
129|# Agent Skills specification constraints (https://agentskills.io/specification)
130|MAX_SKILL_NAME_LENGTH = 64
131|MAX_SKILL_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH = 1024
132|MAX_SKILL_COMPATIBILITY_LENGTH = 500
133|
134|
135|class SkillMetadata(TypedDict):
136|    """Metadata for a skill per Agent Skills specification (https://agentskills.io/specification)."""
137|
138|    path: str
139|    """Path to the SKILL.md file."""
140|
141|    name: str
142|    """Skill identifier.
143|
144|    Constraints per Agent Skills specification:
145|
146|    - 1-64 characters
147|    - Unicode lowercase alphanumeric and hyphens only (`a-z` and `-`).
148|    - Must not start or end with `-`
149|    - Must not contain consecutive `--`
150|    - Must match the parent directory name containing the `SKILL.md` file
151|    """
152|
153|    description: str
154|    """What the skill does.
155|
156|    Constraints per Agent Skills specification:
157|
158|    - 1-1024 characters
159|    - Should describe both what the skill does and when to use it
160|    - Should include specific keywords that help agents identify relevant tasks
161|    """
162|
163|    license: str | None
164|    """License name or reference to bundled license file."""
165|
166|    compatibility: str | None
167|    """Environment requirements.
168|
169|    Constraints per Agent Skills specification:
170|
171|    - 1-500 characters if provided
172|    - Should only be included if there are specific compatibility requirements
173|    - Can indicate intended product, required packages, etc.
174|    """
175|
176|    metadata: dict[str, str]
177|    """Arbitrary key-value mapping for additional metadata.
178|
179|    Clients can use this to store additional properties not defined by the spec.
180|
181|    It is recommended to keep key names unique to avoid conflicts.
182|    """
183|
184|    allowed_tools: list[str]
185|    """Tool names the skill recommends using.
186|
187|    Warning: this is experimental.
188|
189|    Constraints per Agent Skills specification:
190|
191|    - Space-delimited list of tool names
192|    """
193|
194|
195|class SkillsState(AgentState):
196|    """State for the skills middleware."""
197|
198|    skills_metadata: NotRequired[Annotated[list[SkillMetadata], PrivateStateAttr]]
199|    """List of loaded skill metadata from configured sources. Not propagated to parent agents."""
200|
201|
202|class SkillsStateUpdate(TypedDict):
203|    """State update for the skills middleware."""
204|
205|    skills_metadata: list[SkillMetadata]
206|    """List of loaded skill metadata to merge into state."""
207|
208|
209|def _validate_skill_name(name: str, directory_name: str) -> tuple[bool, str]:
210|    """Validate skill name per Agent Skills specification.
211|
212|    Constraints per Agent Skills specification:
213|
214|    - 1-64 characters
215|    - Unicode lowercase alphanumeric and hyphens only (`a-z` and `-`).
216|    - Must not start or end with `-`
217|    - Must not contain consecutive `--`
218|    - Must match the parent directory name containing the `SKILL.md` file
219|
220|    Unicode lowercase alphanumeric means any character where `c.isalpha() and
221|    c.islower()` or `c.isdigit()` returns `True`, which covers accented Latin
222|    characters (e.g., `'café'`, `'über-tool'`) and other scripts.
223|
224|    Args:
225|        name: Skill name from YAML frontmatter
226|        directory_name: Parent directory name
227|
228|    Returns:
229|        `(is_valid, error_message)` tuple.
230|
231|            Error message is empty if valid.
232|    """
233|    if not name:
234|        return False, "name is required"
235|    if len(name) > MAX_SKILL_NAME_LENGTH:
236|        return False, "name exceeds 64 characters"
237|    if name.startswith("-") or name.endswith("-") or "--" in name:
238|        return False, "name must be lowercase alphanumeric with single hyphens only"
239|    for c in name:
240|        if c == "-":
241|            continue
242|        if (c.isalpha() and c.islower()) or c.isdigit():
243|            continue
244|        return False, "name must be lowercase alphanumeric with single hyphens only"
245|    if name != directory_name:
246|        return False, f"name '{name}' must match directory name '{directory_name}'"
247|    return True, ""
248|
249|
250|def _parse_skill_metadata(  # noqa: C901
251|        content: str,
252|        skill_path: str,
253|        directory_name: str,
254|) -> SkillMetadata | None:
255|    """Parse YAML frontmatter from `SKILL.md` content.
256|
257|    Extracts metadata per Agent Skills specification from YAML frontmatter
258|    delimited by `---` markers at the start of the content.
259|
260|    Args:
261|        content: Content of the `SKILL.md` file
262|        skill_path: Path to the `SKILL.md` file (for error messages and metadata)
263|        directory_name: Name of the parent directory containing the skill
264|
265|    Returns:
266|        `SkillMetadata` if parsing succeeds, `None` if parsing fails or
267|            validation errors occur
268|    """
269|    if len(content) > MAX_SKILL_FILE_SIZE:
270|        logger.warning("Skipping %s: content too large (%d bytes)", skill_path, len(content))
271|        return None
272|
273|    # Match YAML frontmatter between --- delimiters
274|    frontmatter_pattern = r"^---\s*\n(.*?)\n---\s*\n"
275|    match = re.match(frontmatter_pattern, content, re.DOTALL)
276|
277|    if not match:
278|        logger.warning("Skipping %s: no valid YAML frontmatter found", skill_path)
279|        return None
280|
281|    frontmatter_str = match.group(1)
282|
283|    # Parse YAML using safe_load for proper nested structure support
284|    try:
285|        frontmatter_data = yaml.safe_load(frontmatter_str)
286|    except yaml.YAMLError as e:
287|        logger.warning("Invalid YAML in %s: %s", skill_path, e)
288|        return None
289|
290|    if not isinstance(frontmatter_data, dict):
291|        logger.warning("Skipping %s: frontmatter is not a mapping", skill_path)
292|        return None
293|
294|    name = str(frontmatter_data.get("name", "")).strip()
295|    description = str(frontmatter_data.get("description", "")).strip()
296|    if not name or not description:
297|        logger.warning("Skipping %s: missing required 'name' or 'description'", skill_path)
298|        return None
299|
300|    # Validate name format per spec (warn but continue loading for backwards compatibility)
301|    is_valid, error = _validate_skill_name(str(name), directory_name)
302|    if not is_valid:
303|        logger.warning(
304|            "Skill '%s' in %s does not follow Agent Skills specification: %s. Consider renaming for spec compliance.",
305|            name,
306|            skill_path,
307|            error,
308|        )
309|
310|    description_str = description
311|    if len(description_str) > MAX_SKILL_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH:
312|        logger.warning(
313|            "Description exceeds %d characters in %s, truncating",
314|            MAX_SKILL_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH,
315|            skill_path,
316|        )
317|        description_str = description_str[:MAX_SKILL_DESCRIPTION_LENGTH]
318|
319|    raw_tools = frontmatter_data.get("allowed-tools")
320|    if isinstance(raw_tools, str):
321|        allowed_tools = [
322|            t.strip(",")  # Support commas for compatibility with skills created for Claude Code.
323|            for t in raw_tools.split()
324|            if t.strip(",")
325|        ]
326|    else:
327|        if raw_tools is not None:
328|            logger.warning(
329|                "Ignoring non-string 'allowed-tools' in %s (got %s)",
330|                skill_path,
331|                type(raw_tools).__name__,
332|            )
333|        allowed_tools = []
334|
335|    compatibility_str = str(frontmatter_data.get("compatibility", "")).strip() or None
336|    if compatibility_str and len(compatibility_str) > MAX_SKILL_COMPATIBILITY_LENGTH:
337|        logger.warning(
338|            "Compatibility exceeds %d characters in %s, truncating",
339|            MAX_SKILL_COMPATIBILITY_LENGTH,
340|            skill_path,
341|        )
342|        compatibility_str = compatibility_str[:MAX_SKILL_COMPATIBILITY_LENGTH]
343|
344|    return SkillMetadata(
345|        name=str(name),
346|        description=description_str,
347|        path=skill_path,
348|        metadata=_validate_metadata(frontmatter_data.get("metadata", {}), skill_path),
349|        license=str(frontmatter_data.get("license", "")).strip() or None,
350|        compatibility=compatibility_str,
351|        allowed_tools=allowed_tools,
352|    )
353|
354|
355|def _validate_metadata(
356|        raw: object,
357|        skill_path: str,
358|) -> dict[str, str]:
359|    """Validate and normalize the metadata field from YAML frontmatter.
360|
361|    YAML `safe_load` can return any type for the `metadata` key. This
362|    ensures the values in `SkillMetadata` are always a `dict[str, str]` by
363|    coercing via `str()` and rejecting non-dict inputs.
364|
365|    Args:
366|        raw: Raw value from `frontmatter_data.get("metadata", {})`.
367|        skill_path: Path to the `SKILL.md` file (for warning messages).
368|
369|    Returns:
370|        A validated `dict[str, str]`.
371|    """
372|    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
373|        if raw:
374|            logger.warning(
375|                "Ignoring non-dict metadata in %s (got %s)",
376|                skill_path,
377|                type(raw).__name__,
378|            )
379|        return {}
380|    return {str(k): str(v) for k, v in raw.items()}
381|
382|
383|def _format_skill_annotations(skill: SkillMetadata) -> str:
384|    """Build a parenthetical annotation string from optional skill fields.
385|
386|    Combines license and compatibility into a comma-separated string for
387|    display in the system prompt skill listing.
388|
389|    Args:
390|        skill: Skill metadata to extract annotations from.
391|
392|    Returns:
393|        Annotation string like `'License: MIT, Compatibility: Python 3.10+'`,
394|            or empty string if neither field is set.
395|    """
396|    parts: list[str] = []
397|    if skill.get("license"):
398|        parts.append(f"License: {skill['license']}")
399|    if skill.get("compatibility"):
400|        parts.append(f"Compatibility: {skill['compatibility']}")
401|    return ", ".join(parts)
402|
403|
404|def _list_skills(backend: BackendProtocol, source_path: str) -> list[SkillMetadata]:
405|    """List all skills from a backend source.
406|
407|    Scans backend for subdirectories containing `SKILL.md` files, downloads
408|    their content, parses YAML frontmatter, and returns skill metadata.
409|
410|    Expected structure:
411|
412|    ```txt
413|    source_path/
414|    └── skill-name/
415|        ├── SKILL.md   # Required
416|        └── helper.py  # Optional
417|    ```
418|
419|    Args:
420|        backend: Backend instance to use for file operations
421|        source_path: Path to the skills directory in the backend
422|
423|    Returns:
424|        List of skill metadata from successfully parsed `SKILL.md` files
425|    """
426|    skills: list[SkillMetadata] = []
427|    ls_result = backend.ls(source_path)
428|    items = ls_result.entries if isinstance(ls_result, LsResult) else ls_result
429|
430|    # Find all skill directories (directories containing SKILL.md)
431|    skill_dirs = []
432|    for item in items or []:
433|        if not item.get("is_dir"):
434|            continue
435|        skill_dirs.append(item["path"])
436|
437|    if not skill_dirs:
438|        return []
439|
440|    # For each skill directory, check if SKILL.md exists and download it
441|    skill_md_paths = []
442|    for skill_dir_path in skill_dirs:
443|        # Construct SKILL.md path using PurePosixPath for safe, standardized path operations
444|        skill_dir = PurePosixPath(skill_dir_path)
445|        skill_md_path = str(skill_dir / "SKILL.md")
446|        skill_md_paths.append((skill_dir_path, skill_md_path))
447|
448|    paths_to_download = [skill_md_path for _, skill_md_path in skill_md_paths]
449|    responses = backend.download_files(paths_to_download)
450|
451|    # Parse each downloaded SKILL.md
452|    for (skill_dir_path, skill_md_path), response in zip(skill_md_paths, responses, strict=True):
453|        if response.error:
454|            # Skill doesn't have a SKILL.md, skip it
455|            continue
456|
457|        if response.content is None:
458|            logger.warning("Downloaded skill file %s has no content", skill_md_path)
459|            continue
460|
461|        try:
462|            content = response.content.decode("utf-8")
463|        except UnicodeDecodeError as e:
464|            logger.warning("Error decoding %s: %s", skill_md_path, e)
465|            continue
466|
467|        # Extract directory name from path using PurePosixPath
468|        directory_name = PurePosixPath(skill_dir_path).name
469|
470|        # Parse metadata
471|        skill_metadata = _parse_skill_metadata(
472|            content=content,
473|            skill_path=skill_md_path,
474|            directory_name=directory_name,
475|        )
476|        if skill_metadata:
477|            skills.append(skill_metadata)
478|
479|    return skills
480|
481|
482|async def _alist_skills(backend: BackendProtocol, source_path: str) -> list[SkillMetadata]:
483|    """List all skills from a backend source (async version).
484|
485|    Scans backend for subdirectories containing `SKILL.md` files, downloads
486|    their content, parses YAML frontmatter, and returns skill metadata.
487|
488|    Expected structure:
489|
490|    ```txt
491|    source_path/
492|    └── skill-name/
493|        ├── SKILL.md   # Required
494|        └── helper.py  # Optional
495|    ```
496|
497|    Args:
498|        backend: Backend instance to use for file operations
499|        source_path: Path to the skills directory in the backend
500|
501|    Returns:
502|        List of skill metadata from successfully parsed `SKILL.md` files
503|    """
504|    skills: list[SkillMetadata] = []
505|    ls_result = await backend.als(source_path)
506|    items = ls_result.entries if isinstance(ls_result, LsResult) else ls_result
507|
508|    # Find all skill directories (directories containing SKILL.md)
509|    skill_dirs = []
510|    for item in items or []:
511|        if not item.get("is_dir"):
512|            continue
513|        skill_dirs.append(item["path"])
514|
515|    if not skill_dirs:
516|        return []
517|
518|    # For each skill directory, check if SKILL.md exists and download it
519|    skill_md_paths = []
520|    for skill_dir_path in skill_dirs:
521|        # Construct SKILL.md path using PurePosixPath for safe, standardized path operations
522|        skill_dir = PurePosixPath(skill_dir_path)
523|        skill_md_path = str(skill_dir / "SKILL.md")
524|        skill_md_paths.append((skill_dir_path, skill_md_path))
525|
526|    paths_to_download = [skill_md_path for _, skill_md_path in skill_md_paths]
527|    responses = await backend.adownload_files(paths_to_download)
528|
529|    # Parse each downloaded SKILL.md
530|    for (skill_dir_path, skill_md_path), response in zip(skill_md_paths, responses, strict=True):
531|        if response.error:
532|            # Skill doesn't have a SKILL.md, skip it
533|            continue
534|
535|        if response.content is None:
536|            logger.warning("Downloaded skill file %s has no content", skill_md_path)
537|            continue
538|
539|        try:
540|            content = response.content.decode("utf-8")
541|        except UnicodeDecodeError as e:
542|            logger.warning("Error decoding %s: %s", skill_md_path, e)
543|            continue
544|
545|        # Extract directory name from path using PurePosixPath
546|        directory_name = PurePosixPath(skill_dir_path).name
547|
548|        # Parse metadata
549|        skill_metadata = _parse_skill_metadata(
550|            content=content,
551|            skill_path=skill_md_path,
552|            directory_name=directory_name,
553|        )
554|        if skill_metadata:
555|            skills.append(skill_metadata)
556|
557|    return skills
